    var popup;
    var ouibounceCallback = function () {
        $TB.popoverShown('popover-lm');
    };
    try {
        jQuery.getScript("//cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/ouibounce/0.0.11/ouibounce.min.js", function (data, textStatus, jqxhr) {
            popup = ouibounce(document.getElementById("fr_popover_overlay"), {
                cookieExpire: 30,
                cookieName: "newsPopup",
                callback: ouibounceCallback,
                sitewide: true,
                aggressive: false,
                timer: 1000,
                
                delay: 3600000,
                
            });
            
        });
